Actions on Objectives
Detected
Reconnaissance. Attacker researches the target — OSINT, scanning, enumeration.
Weaponization. Coupling exploit code with deliverable payload (e.g. weaponized PDF/Office doc, trojanized installer).
Delivery. Transmitting the weapon (phishing email, USB, watering hole, drive-by, malicious ad). → [LLM] DreamJob trojanized PDF/installer execution from job-lure decoy folder
Exploitation. Triggering the exploit — vulnerability/macro/trust abuse to execute code. → PowerShell encoded / obfuscated command · Trusted vendor binary / installer launching unusual children · Article-specific behavioural hunt — Gotta fly: Lazarus targets the UAV sector
Installation. Establishing persistence on the host — backdoor, service, scheduled task, registry key. → [LLM] Lazarus DreamJob DLL side-load: wksprt.exe loading webservices.dll from ProgramData · [LLM] Lazarus DreamJob loader/dropper SHA1 sighting (DroneEXEHijackingLoader / NukeSped family) · File hash IOCs — endpoint file/process match
Command & Control. Beacon to attacker infrastructure for control and tasking. → [LLM] ScoringMathTea C2 beacon to compromised WordPress hosts (Lazarus DreamJob IOCs) · Beaconing — periodic outbound to small set of destinations · Network connections to article IPs / domains
Actions on Objectives. Lateral movement, credential dumping, data theft, ransomware, sabotage.
[LLM] Lazarus DreamJob DLL side-load: wksprt.exe loading webservices.dll from ProgramData Installation High
Detects the Operation DreamJob side-loading chain where the legitimate Windows Remote Desktop binary wksprt.exe is run from a non-standard ProgramData subfolder (EMC\, Adobe\) and loads a malicious webservices.dll dropped alongside it. This hijack pattern delivers the ScoringMathTea RAT. Rationale: Pivots on the Table 1 side-load chain (wksprt.exe→webservices.dll, wkspbroker.exe→radcui.dll) AND on the article's anomalous host paths (%ALLUSERSPROFILE%\EMC\, \Adobe\, %APPDATA%\Microsoft\RemoteApp\). Legitimate Microsoft Remote Desktop binaries should never execute from these locations. Cross-checked against: • https://attack.mitre.org/groups/G0032/ • https://www.clearskysec.com/operation-dream-job/
ATT&CKT1574.002 T1574.001
Data sourcesEndpoint.Processes Endpoint.Filesystem
let suspect_paths = dynamic([@"\programdata\emc\", @"\programdata\adobe\", @"\appdata\roaming\microsoft\remoteapp\"]);
let host_proc = DeviceProcessEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(30d)
| where FileName in~ ("wksprt.exe", "wkspbroker.exe")
| where FolderPath has_any (suspect_paths) or FolderPath !startswith @"C:\Windows\"
| project Timestamp, DeviceId, DeviceName, AccountName, ProcessId = tostring(ProcessId), FileName, FolderPath, ProcessCommandLine, SHA256;
let host_lib = DeviceImageLoadEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(30d)
| where FileName in~ ("webservices.dll", "radcui.dll", "libmupdf.dll", "libpcre.dll", "hidefirstletter.dll", "tsmsisrv.dll")
| where FolderPath has_any (suspect_paths) or FolderPath !startswith @"C:\Windows\"
| project Timestamp, DeviceId, DllName = FileName, DllPath = FolderPath, DllSha256 = SHA256, InitiatingProcessFileName, InitiatingProcessId = tostring(InitiatingProcessId);
host_proc
| join kind=inner host_lib on DeviceId, $left.ProcessId == $right.InitiatingProcessId
| where InitiatingProcessFileName =~ FileName
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, FileName, FolderPath, DllName, DllPath, DllSha256, ProcessCommandLine, SHA256
| order by Timestamp desc
let suspect_paths = dynamic([@"\programdata\emc\", @"\programdata\adobe\", @"\appdata\roaming\microsoft\remoteapp\"]);
let bad_dlls = dynamic(["webservices.dll","radcui.dll","libmupdf.dll","libpcre.dll","hidefirstletter.dll","tsmsisrv.dll"]);
ImProcessCreate
| where TimeGenerated > ago(30d)
| where ActingProcessName has_any ("wksprt.exe","wkspbroker.exe")
| where ActingProcessName has_any (suspect_paths) or not(ActingProcessName startswith @"C:\Windows\")
| project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname, ActorUsername, ActingProcessName, ActingProcessCommandLine, SHA256
| union (SecurityEvent
    | where TimeGenerated > ago(30d)
    | where EventID == 4688
    | where NewProcessName has_any ("wksprt.exe","wkspbroker.exe")

    | project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname=Computer, ActorUsername=Account, ActingProcessName=NewProcessName, ActingProcessCommandLine=CommandLine, SHA256=tostring(parse_xml(EventData).EventData.Data[10]))
| order by TimeGenerated desc
title: Lazarus DreamJob — wksprt.exe Side-load from ProgramData
id: 7e1a4d2c-3f60-4d6a-9b8a-9f6cf2a2b401
description: Detects Operation DreamJob DLL side-loading where wksprt.exe / wkspbroker.exe runs from a ProgramData or RemoteApp subfolder instead of System32, signalling deployment of webservices.dll / radcui.dll loader.
references:
  - https://www.welivesecurity.com/en/eset-research/gotta-fly-lazarus-targets-uav-sector/
author: SOC Detection Engineering
date: 2026/05/21
tags:
  - attack.t1574.002
  - attack.t1574.001
  - attack.g0032
logsource:
  category: process_creation
  product: windows
detection:
  selection_proc:
    Image|endswith:
      - '\wksprt.exe'
      - '\wkspbroker.exe'
  selection_path:
    Image|contains:
      - '\ProgramData\EMC\'
      - '\ProgramData\Adobe\'
      - '\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\RemoteApp\'
  filter_legit:
    Image|startswith: 'C:\Windows\'
  condition: selection_proc and selection_path and not filter_legit
level: high
source:(windows.sysmon OR windows.security) (@EventID:(1 OR 4688)) (@Image:(*\\wksprt.exe OR *\\Wksprt.exe OR *\\WKSPRT.EXE OR *\\wkspbroker.exe OR *\\WkspBroker.exe OR *\\WKSPBROKER.EXE) OR @NewProcessName:(*\\wksprt.exe OR *\\wkspbroker.exe)) (@Image:(*\\ProgramData\\EMC\\* OR *\\ProgramData\\Adobe\\* OR *\\RemoteApp\\*) OR @NewProcessName:(*\\ProgramData\\EMC\\* OR *\\ProgramData\\Adobe\\* OR *\\RemoteApp\\*))
#event_simpleName=ProcessRollup2
| ImageFileName=/\\(wksprt|wkspbroker)\.exe$/i
| regex("\\\\(ProgramData\\\\(EMC|Adobe)|AppData\\\\Roaming\\\\Microsoft\\\\RemoteApp)\\\\", field=ImageFileName)
| NOT (ImageFileName=/^C:\\Windows\\/i)
| groupBy([aid, ComputerName, UserName, ImageFileName, CommandLine, SHA256HashData], function=count())
ENDPOINT

[LLM] ScoringMathTea C2 beacon to compromised WordPress hosts (Lazarus DreamJob IOCs) Command & Control High
Detects egress to the Operation DreamJob C&C infrastructure that hosts ScoringMathTea controllers — compromised WordPress sites where the server-side handler lives under /wp-content/themes/ or /wp-content/plugins/. Targets the eight ESET-disclosed domains and eight resolved IPs. Rationale: Uses the verbatim domain/IP IOC bundle published by ESET for this campaign — each is a compromised WordPress server hosting ScoringMathTea's server-side handler, so any beacon from a corporate host is high-confidence malicious. Cross-checked against: • https://www.welivesecurity.com/en/eset-research/gotta-fly-lazarus-targets-uav-sector/ • https://attack.mitre.org/groups/G0032/
ATT&CKT1071.001 T1102 T1584.004

let c2_ips = dynamic(["23.111.133.162","104.21.80.1","70.32.24.131","185.148.129.24","66.29.144.75","108.181.92.71","104.247.162.67","193.39.187.165"]);

[LLM] Lazarus DreamJob loader/dropper SHA1 sighting (DroneEXEHijackingLoader / NukeSped family) Installation High
Hash-based sweep for the exact loader and dropper artefacts published by ESET (Win64/NukeSped.TL/TE/TO/TP) — QuanPinLoader, libmupdf.dll loader, radcui.dll dropper, HideFirstLetter.DLL (BinMergeLoader) and libpcre.dll loader. Any sighting in file write / image load / process telemetry is a confirmed compromise. Rationale: Uses the ESET-published SHA1 set (8 hashes) and the pre-extracted SHA256 set (5 hashes) from the campaign IOC bundle. Hash matches on TSMSISrv.dll, libmupdf.dll, radcui.dll, HideFirstLetter.DLL, libpcre.dll have zero benign collisions because they are trojanized versions of legitimate libraries. [LLM] DreamJob trojanized PDF/installer execution from job-lure decoy folder Delivery Medium
Targets the Operation DreamJob initial-access pattern: the user opens a 'job description' decoy and runs a trojanized PDF reader or open-source tool (MuPDF, ComparePlus, WinMerge derivative, libpcre wrapper) that side-loads a malicious DLL. Detection pivots on user-writable download/extract directories spawning Microsoft Workspace binaries that load non-Microsoft DLLs. Rationale: Mirrors the article's narrative — victim receives a job-description archive, extracts via WinRAR/7-Zip/Explorer, and runs a trojanized PDF reader or compare tool that masquerades as / side-loads against Microsoft Workspace executables. Adds the 'parent is unpacker or browser/mail' constraint that distinguishes user-driven execution from legitimate RDS lifecycle activity. Cross-checked against: • https://www.welivesecurity.com/en/eset-research/gotta-fly-lazarus-targets-uav-sector/ • https://attack.mitre.org/groups/G0032/
ATT&CKT1566.002 T1204.002 T1036.005
Data sourcesEndpoint.Processes
DeviceProcessEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(14d)
| where FileName in~ ("wksprt.exe","wkspbroker.exe")
| where InitiatingProcessFileName in~ ("explorer.exe","7zg.exe","7zfm.exe","winrar.exe","rar.exe","chrome.exe","msedge.exe","firefox.exe","outlook.exe","thunderbird.exe")
| where FolderPath has_any (@"\users\", @"\downloads\", @"\desktop\", @"\temp\", @"\programdata\")
| where FolderPath !startswith @"C:\Windows\"
| join kind=inner (
    DeviceImageLoadEvents
    | where Timestamp > ago(14d)
    | where FileName in~ ("webservices.dll","radcui.dll","libmupdf.dll","libpcre.dll","hidefirstletter.dll","tsmsisrv.dll")
    | where InitiatingProcessVersionInfoCompanyName !has "Microsoft"
    | project DeviceId, Timestamp_lib=Timestamp, DllName=FileName, DllPath=FolderPath, DllSha256=SHA256, InitiatingProcessId
) on DeviceId, $left.ProcessId == $right.InitiatingProcessId
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, InitiatingProcessFileName, ParentExec=InitiatingProcessFileName, ExecPath=FolderPath, DllName, DllPath, DllSha256, ProcessCommandLine
| order by Timestamp desc
ImProcessCreate
| where TimeGenerated > ago(14d)
| where ActingProcessName has_any ("wksprt.exe","wkspbroker.exe")
| where ParentProcessName has_any ("explorer.exe","7zg.exe","7zfm.exe","winrar.exe","rar.exe","chrome.exe","msedge.exe","firefox.exe","outlook.exe","thunderbird.exe")
| where ActingProcessName has_any (@"\users\",@"\downloads\",@"\desktop\",@"\temp\",@"\programdata\")
| where not(ActingProcessName startswith @"C:\Windows\")
| project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname, ActorUsername, ParentProcessName, ActingProcessName, ActingProcessCommandLine, SHA256
| order by TimeGenerated desc
title: Lazarus DreamJob — Workspace Binary Spawned by Archive Extractor or Browser from User Folder
id: c2d1f3b9-9b34-4c8b-bd33-50e1f7a8c903
description: Job-lure initial-access pattern — a trojanized archive or download causes wksprt.exe / wkspbroker.exe to execute from a user folder under explorer.exe or an archive tool, indicating Operation DreamJob deployment.
references:
  - https://www.welivesecurity.com/en/eset-research/gotta-fly-lazarus-targets-uav-sector/
author: SOC Detection Engineering
date: 2026/05/21
tags:
  - attack.t1566.002
  - attack.t1204.002
  - attack.t1036.005
logsource:
  category: process_creation
  product: windows
detection:
  selection_child:
    Image|endswith:
      - '\wksprt.exe'
      - '\wkspbroker.exe'
  selection_parent:
    ParentImage|endswith:
      - '\explorer.exe'
      - '\7zg.exe'
      - '\7zfm.exe'
      - '\winrar.exe'
      - '\rar.exe'
      - '\chrome.exe'
      - '\msedge.exe'
      - '\firefox.exe'
      - '\outlook.exe'
  selection_path:
    Image|contains:
      - '\Users\'
      - '\Downloads\'
      - '\Temp\'
      - '\ProgramData\'
  filter_legit:
    Image|startswith: 'C:\Windows\'
  condition: selection_child and selection_parent and selection_path and not filter_legit
level: high

#event_simpleName=ProcessRollup2
| ImageFileName=/\\(wksprt|wkspbroker)\.exe$/i
| ParentBaseFileName=/^(explorer|7zg|7zfm|winrar|rar|chrome|msedge|firefox|outlook|thunderbird)\.exe$/i

| NOT (ImageFileName=/^C:\\Windows\\/i)
| groupBy([aid, ComputerName, UserName, ParentBaseFileName, ImageFileName, CommandLine, SHA256HashData], function=count())

Beaconing — periodic outbound to small set of destinations Command & Control Medium
C2 channel detection via inter-beacon-time stddev / fan-out to single dest.
ATT&CKT1071.001 T1071.004
Data sourcesNetwork_Traffic.All_Traffic DeviceNetworkEvents
DeviceNetworkEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(1d)
| where RemoteIPType == "Public" and ActionType == "ConnectionSuccess"
| project DeviceName, RemoteIP, RemotePort, Timestamp
| sort by DeviceName asc, RemoteIP asc, RemotePort asc, Timestamp asc
| extend prev_dev = prev(DeviceName, 1), prev_ip = prev(RemoteIP, 1),
         prev_port = prev(RemotePort, 1), prev_ts = prev(Timestamp, 1)
| where DeviceName == prev_dev and RemoteIP == prev_ip and RemotePort == prev_port
| extend delta_sec = datetime_diff('second', Timestamp, prev_ts)
| summarize conn_count = count(), avg_delta = avg(delta_sec), stdev_delta = stdev(delta_sec)
    by DeviceName, RemoteIP, RemotePort
| where conn_count > 30 and avg_delta between (30.0 .. 600.0) and stdev_delta < 5.0
| order by conn_count desc
ImNetworkSession
| where TimeGenerated > ago(2h)
| where DvcAction != "Block"
| where ipv4_is_private(DstIpAddr) == false
| summarize ConnCount = count(),
            DistinctMinutes = dcount(bin(TimeGenerated, 1m))
            by DvcHostname, DstIpAddr, DstPortNumber
| where ConnCount > 50 and DistinctMinutes > 30   // sustained, not bursty
| order by ConnCount desc
NETWORK
Toggle if your env has no CIM data-model acceleration.
| tstats `summariesonly` count, values(All_Traffic.dest_port) AS ports
    from datamodel=Network_Traffic.All_Traffic
    where All_Traffic.action="allowed" AND All_Traffic.dest_category!="internal"
    by _time span=10s, All_Traffic.src, All_Traffic.dest
| `drop_dm_object_name(All_Traffic)`
| streamstats current=f last(_time) AS prev_time by src, dest
| eval delta = _time - prev_time
| stats avg(delta) AS avg_delta stdev(delta) AS sd_delta count by src, dest
| where count > 30 AND sd_delta < 5 AND avg_delta>=30 AND avg_delta<=600
| sort - count
| tstats summariesonly=false count, values(All_Traffic.dest_port) AS ports
    from datamodel=Network_Traffic.All_Traffic
    where All_Traffic.action="allowed" AND All_Traffic.dest_category!="internal"
    by _time span=10s, All_Traffic.src, All_Traffic.dest
| `drop_dm_object_name(All_Traffic)`
| streamstats current=f last(_time) AS prev_time by src, dest
| eval delta = _time - prev_time
| stats avg(delta) AS avg_delta stdev(delta) AS sd_delta count by src, dest
| where count > 30 AND sd_delta < 5 AND avg_delta>=30 AND avg_delta<=600
| sort - count

PowerShell encoded / obfuscated command Exploitation High
Encoded or obfuscated PowerShell — common across loaders, recon, and post-exploitation.
ATT&CKT1059.001 T1027
Data sourcesEndpoint.Processes DeviceProcessEvents
DeviceProcessEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(7d)
| where AccountName !endswith "$"
| where FileName in~ ("powershell.exe","pwsh.exe")
| where ProcessCommandLine matches regex @"(?i)(-enc|encodedcommand|frombase64string|-nop|-w\s+hidden|invoke-expression|iex\s*\(|downloadstring|net\.webclient)"
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, ProcessCommandLine,
          InitiatingProcessFileName, InitiatingProcessCommandLine
SecurityEvent
| where TimeGenerated > ago(7d)
| where EventID == 4688
| where Account !endswith "$"
| where NewProcessName endswith "\powershell.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\pwsh.exe"
| where CommandLine matches regex @"(?i)(-enc|encodedcommand|frombase64string|-nop|-w\s+hidden|invoke-expression|iex\s*\(|downloadstring|net\.webclient)"
| project TimeGenerated, Computer, Account, NewProcessName,
          CommandLine, ParentProcessName
| order by TimeGenerated desc

title: Obfuscated / Encoded PowerShell Execution
id: 7c8b2e10-4d4f-4a8c-9c1f-bf6e2c4a8d08
status: experimental
description: |
    PowerShell launched with -EncodedCommand, base64 strings, hidden
    window, no-profile, bypass policy, IEX / Invoke-Expression / Net
    .WebClient.DownloadString — combinations indicating evasion.
references:
    - https://attack.mitre.org/techniques/T1059/001/
    - https://attack.mitre.org/techniques/T1027/
author: Clankerusecase
date: 2026/05/02
tags:
    - attack.t1059.001
    - attack.t1027
    - attack.execution
    - attack.defense-evasion
logsource:
    category: process_creation
    product: windows
detection:
    selection_image:
        Image|endswith:
            - '\powershell.exe'
            - '\pwsh.exe'
    selection_args:
        CommandLine|contains:
            - '-enc '
            - 'EncodedCommand'
            - 'FromBase64String'
            - 'Invoke-Expression'
            - 'IEX('
            - 'DownloadString'
            - 'Net.WebClient'
            - '-w hidden'
            - '-WindowStyle Hidden'
            - '-nop'
            - '-NoProfile'
            - '-ep bypass'
            - '-ExecutionPolicy Bypass'
    condition: selection_image and selection_args
falsepositives:
    - DevOps tooling that legitimately uses encoded command for
      cross-shell escaping
level: high

        OR Processes.process="*FromBase64String*" OR Processes.process="*-nop*"
        OR Processes.process="*-w hidden*" OR Processes.process="*Invoke-Expression*"
        OR Processes.process="*IEX(*" OR Processes.process="*DownloadString*"
        OR Processes.process="*Net.WebClient*")

ATT&CKT1195.002

DeviceProcessEvents

| where InitiatingProcessFileName in~ ("setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe")
| where FileName in~ ("powershell.exe","cmd.exe","rundll32.exe","regsvr32.exe","mshta.exe","wscript.exe","cscript.exe","wmic.exe","bitsadmin.exe")
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, InitiatingProcessFileName, FileName, ProcessCommandLine
// Replace `"setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe"` with the vendor binaries observed in the
// article (matching shape with the Defender query).
SecurityEvent
| where TimeGenerated > ago(7d)
| where EventID == 4688
| where Account !endswith "$"
| where ParentProcessName has_any ("setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe")
| where NewProcessName endswith "\powershell.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\cmd.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\rundll32.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\regsvr32.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\mshta.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\wscript.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\cscript.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\wmic.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\bitsadmin.exe"
| project TimeGenerated, Computer, Account, ParentProcessName,
          NewProcessName, CommandLine
| order by TimeGenerated desc

        Image|endswith:
            - '\powershell.exe'
            - '\pwsh.exe'

            - '\mshta.exe'
            - '\wscript.exe'
            - '\cscript.exe'
            - '\wmic.exe'

            - '\Program Files\'
            - '\Program Files (x86)\'

// Pre-filter both ASIM tables before the union — same pattern as the
// Defender version so the engine can push hash predicates per-side.
let BadHashes = dynamic(["c39ecc7d9f1e225a37304345731fffe72cdb95b21aeb06aa6022f6d338777012", "083d4a4ef6267c9a0ab57f1e5a2ed45ff67a0b4db83bbd43563458a223781120", "503b3ece42f540409bcb2f0abc7584e557a0d120b7ba9854b4548496b2546d34", "98d1a10521a4dd968d75e2860e523311b5851737795c84943c380870794c851a", "f9a9c1a13ed74aebca0652b102755833fc084e221d731b5e7ae76ff136f85864", "28978E987BC59E75CA22562924EAB93355CF679E", "5E5BBA521F0034D342CC26DB8BCFECE57DBD4616", "B12EEB595FEEC2CFBF9A60E1CC21A14CE8873539", "26AA2643B07C48CB6943150ADE541580279E8E0E", "0CB73D70FD4132A4FF5493DAA84AAE839F6329D5", "03D9B8F0FCF9173D2964CE7173D21E681DFA8DA4", "71D0DDB7C6CAC4BA2BDE679941FA92A31FBEC1FF", "87B2DF764455164C6982BA9700F27EA34D3565DF", "E670C4275EC24D403E0D4DE7135CBCF1D54FF09C", "5B85DD485FD516AA1F4412801897A40A9BE31837", "B68C49841DC48E3672031795D85ED24F9F619782", "AC16B1BAEDE349E4824335E0993533BF4FC116B3", "2AA341B03FAC3054C57640122EA849BC0C2B6AF6", "CB7834BE7DE07F89352080654F7FEB574B42A2B8", "262B4ED6AC6A977135DECA5B0872B7D6D676083A", "086816466D9D9C12FCADA1C872B8C0FF0A5FC611", "2A2B20FDDD65BA28E7C57AC97A158C15B61A7B05"]);
union
    ( ImFileEvent
        | where TimeGenerated > ago(7d)
        | where ActorUsername !endswith "$"
        | where TargetFileSHA256 in~ (BadHashes)
           or TargetFileSHA1 in~ (BadHashes)
           or TargetFileMD5 in~ (BadHashes)
        | project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname, EventType,
                  TargetFileName, TargetFilePath, TargetFileSHA256,
                  Cmd = ActingProcessName, Source = "ImFileEvent" ),
    ( ImProcessCreate
        | where TimeGenerated > ago(7d)
        | where ActorUsername !endswith "$"
        | where SHA256 in~ (BadHashes) or SHA1 in~ (BadHashes) or MD5 in~ (BadHashes)
        | project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname, EventType = TargetProcessName,
                  TargetFileName = TargetProcessName, TargetFilePath = TargetProcessName,
                  TargetFileSHA256 = SHA256,
                  Cmd = TargetProcessCommandLine, Source = "ImProcessCreate" )
| order by TimeGenerated desc
